2. `models.py:490-520` - where imag_feat flows into critic
3. `models.py:597-665` - the `_imagine` method
4. `models.py:665-680` - `_compute_target` where value is computed

The key insight: dt_embedding is used inside `img_step` of RSSM. It produces `dt_emb` which gets concatenated into the RSSM input, affecting the state representation (`deter`, `stoch`). 

The resulting state features (`imag_feat`) are what the critic sees. So I need to figure out where to block gradients—either by detaching dt_embedding's output during imagination to prevent critic gradients from flowing back through it, or by detaching the contribution more selectively. The challenge is that `_imagine()` calls `img_step` with the dt parameter, and I need to ensure the critic's gradient signal doesn't propagate through the dt_embedding pathway.

Looking more closely at the actual code, I see the critic input is already being detached at line 520 when computing the value loss, so gradients from the critic aren't flowing back through `imag_feat` at all. But the real issue is about world model training—the dt_embedding is part of the world model, and I need to understand whether blocking critic gradients from reaching it is actually the right approach or if there's something else going on with how the world model uses dt information. The dt_embedding flows through the RSSM—it's concatenated with the action and stochastic state, processed through the GRU and deterministic layer, which means the final state representation carries that temporal information. The challenge is isolating this: the imagination features are computed once and shared between actor and critic, so I'd need either to run the imagination twice with different dt_embedding treatments, or find a way to strip the dt information specifically from the critic's feature path.

Actually, stepping back—the gradient flow is what matters here. In DreamerV3, the critic receives detached imagination features and predicts values, so its loss doesn't propagate back to the world model or dt_embedding. The actor then uses those value predictions to compute targets for its own training, while the world model itself learns from reconstruction losses on real data. So the dt_embedding's role in shaping the state representation is preserved for the actor's planning, but the critic operates on frozen features.

The real concern is whether the critic can exploit temporal structure encoded in the features even without gradient flow—the dt_embedding creates a systematic bias in the feature space that the critic learns to predict values from. Since the critic's input is already detached, detaching dt_embedding inside img_step wouldn't affect the critic's training directly. What we're actually testing is whether removing dt information from the features themselves changes the critic's value predictions, which means we need to prevent dt_embedding from influencing the features fed to the critic in the first place.

Looking more carefully at the code, I notice that `_compute_target` calls the value function on `imag_feat` without detaching it—this means gradients can flow backward through the world model during target computation, and the dt_embedding could be influencing those targets in ways that aren't immediately obvious.

{"result":"{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"stdout\": \"# Event-Gated Adaptive-Horizon DreamerV3 — World Model & Behavior\\n# Modified from NM512/dreamerv3-torch.\\n# Changes: WorldModel integrates EventGate + variable-dt sub-sampling;\\n#          ImagBehavior uses gate-driven dt in imagination + adapted lambda-returns.\\n\\nimport copy\\nimport math\\n\\nimport torch\\nfrom torch import nn\\nfrom torch.nn import functional as F\\n\\nimport networks\\nimport tools\\n\\nto_np = lambda x: x.detach().cpu().numpy()\\n\\n\\ndef compute_gate_targets(feats, max_dt, effective_max_dt=None):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Soft supervision target for the event gate derived from state deltas.\\n\\n    Large delta -> prefer small dt (index 0 = dt=1).\\n    Small delta -> prefer large dt (index K-1 = dt=K).\\n    Probability mass is placed only within [1, effective_max_dt].\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    if effective_max_dt is None:\\n        effective_max_dt = max_dt\\n    eff = max(1, int(effective_max_dt))\\n    feats = feats.detach()\\n    deltas = torch.norm(feats[1:] - feats[:-1], dim=-1)  # (time-1, batch)\\n    delta_min = deltas.min(dim=0, keepdim=True).values\\n    delta_max = deltas.max(dim=0, keepdim=True).values\\n    delta_norm = (deltas - delta_min) / (delta_max - delta_min + 1e-8)\\n    preferred_dt = 1.0 + (eff - 1.0) * (1.0 - delta_norm)\\n    dt_indices = torch.arange(1, max_dt + 1, dtype=torch.float32, device=feats.device)\\n    sigma = 1.0\\n    log_probs = -0.5 * ((preferred_dt.unsqueeze(-1) - dt_indices) / sigma) ** 2\\n    if eff < max_dt:\\n        log_probs[..., eff:] = -1e9\\n    target_dist = F.softmax(log_probs, dim=-1)\\n    return target_dist\\n\\n\\ndef gate_loss_fn(gate_logits, target_dist):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Cross-entropy between gate predictions and supervision target.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    log_probs = F.log_softmax(gate_logits, dim=-1)\\n    return -(target_dist * log_probs).sum(dim=-1)\\n\\n\\nclass RewardEMA:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"running mean and std\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\n    def __init__(self, device, alpha=1e-2):\\n        self.device = device\\n        self.alpha = alpha\\n        self.range = torch.tensor([0.05, 0.95], device=device)\\n\\n    def __call__(self, x, ema_vals):\\n        flat_x = torch.flatten(x.detach())\\n        x_quantile = torch.quantile(input=flat_x, q=self.range)\\n        # this should be in-place operation\\n        ema_vals[:] = self.alpha * x_quantile + (1 - self.alpha) * ema_vals\\n        scale = torch.clip(ema_vals[1] - ema_vals[0], min=1.0)\\n        offset = ema_vals[0]\\n        return offset.detach(), scale.detach()\\n\\n\\nclass WorldModel(nn.Module):\\n    def __init__(self, obs_space, act_space, step, config):\\n        super(WorldModel, self).__init__()\\n        self._step = step\\n        self._use_amp = True if config.precision == 16 else False\\n        self._config = config\\n        shapes = {k: tuple(v.shape) for k, v in obs_space.spaces.items()}\\n        self.encoder = networks.MultiEncoder(shapes, **config.encoder)\\n        self.embed_size = self.encoder.outdim\\n        self.dynamics = networks.RSSM(\\n            config.dyn_stoch,\\n            config.dyn_deter,\\n            config.dyn_hidden,\\n            config.dyn_rec_depth,\\n            config.dyn_discrete,\\n            config.act,\\n            config.norm,\\n            config.dyn_mean_act,\\n            config.dyn_std_act,\\n            config.dyn_min_std,\\n            config.unimix_ratio,\\n            config.initial,\\n            config.num_actions,\\n            self.embed_size,\\n            config.device,\\n            max_dt=(2 if getattr(config, \\\"gate_type\\\", \\\"gumbel\\\") == \\\"bernoulli_st\\\" else config.gate_max_dt) if config.gate_enabled else 1,\\n            dt_embed_dim=config.gate_dt_embed_dim,\\n        )\\n        self.heads = nn.ModuleDict()\\n        if config.dyn_discrete:\\n            feat_size = config.dyn_stoch * config.dyn_discrete + config.dyn_deter\\n        else:\\n            feat_size = config.dyn_stoch + config.dyn_deter\\n        self.heads[\\\"decoder\\\"] = networks.MultiDecoder(\\n            feat_size, shapes, **config.decoder\\n        )\\n        self.heads[\\\"reward\\\"] = networks.MLP(\\n            feat_size,\\n            (255,) if config.reward_head[\\\"dist\\\"] == \\\"symlog_disc\\\" else (),\\n            config.reward_h\\n\\n... [截断: 输出过长，省略 22290 字符] ...\\n\\n    gate is not None and self._world_model._gate_scale > 0\\n                and config.gate_fixed_dt == 0\\n            )\\n\\n            def step_fn(prev, _):\\n                state, _, _, prev_dt = prev\\n                feat = dynamics.get_feat(state)\\n                inp = feat.detach()\\n                action = policy(inp).sample()\\n                succ = dynamics.img_step(state, action, dt=prev_dt)\\n                succ_feat = dynamics.get_feat(succ)\\n                if gate_active:\\n                    _, next_dt, _ = gate(succ_feat.detach(), step, hard=True, effective_max_dt=effective_max_dt)\\n                    next_dt = torch.clamp(next_dt, 1, max(1, int(effective_max_dt)))\\n                else:\\n                    batch_size = succ_feat.shape[0]\\n                    fixed = config.gate_fixed_dt if config.gate_fixed_dt > 0 else 1\\n                    next_dt = torch.full(\\n                        (batch_size,), fixed, dtype=torch.long, device=succ_feat.device\\n                    )\\n                return succ, feat, action, next_dt\\n\\n            init_dt = torch.ones(\\n                start[list(start.keys())[0]].shape[0],\\n                dtype=torch.long, device=config.device,\\n            )\\n            if config.gate_fixed_dt > 0:\\n                init_dt = init_dt * config.gate_fixed_dt\\n            succ, feats, actions, dts = tools.static_scan(\\n                step_fn, [torch.arange(horizon)],\\n                (start, None, None, init_dt),\\n            )\\n            states = {\\n                k: torch.cat([start[k][None], v[:-1]], 0)\\n                for k, v in succ.items()\\n            }\\n            return feats, states, actions, dts\\n        else:\\n            def step(prev, _):\\n                state, _, _ = prev\\n                feat = dynamics.get_feat(state)\\n                inp = feat.detach()\\n                action = step, [torch.arange(horizon)], (start, None, None)\\n            )\\n            states = {\\n                k: torch.cat([start[k][None], v[:-1]], 0)\\n                for k, v in succ.items()\\n            }\\n            return feats, states, actions\\n\\n    def _compute_target(self, imag_feat, imag_state, reward, dts=None, init_dt=1.0):\\n        if \\\"cont\\\" in self._world_model.heads:\\n            inp = self._world_model.dynamics.get_feat(imag_state)\\n            discount = self._config.discount * self._world_model.heads[\\\"cont\\\"](inp).mean\\n        else:\\n            discount = self._config.discount * torch.ones_like(reward)\\n        value = self.value(imag_feat).mode()\\n\\n        if dts is not None and self._config.gate_enabled:\\n            H = reward.shape[0]\\n            init_dt_tensor = torch.full_like(dts[:1], init_dt)\\n\\n            horizon_mask = None\\n            if getattr(self._config, 'gate_type', 'gumbel') == 'bernoulli_st':\\n                phys_horizon = getattr(self._config, 'bernoulli_physical_horizon', 30)\\n                if phys_horizon > 0:\\n                    batch_size = dts.shape[1]\\n                    trans_dts_h = torch.cat([\\n                        torch.full((1, batch_size), init_dt, device=dts.device, dtype=torch.float32),\\n                        dts[:H-1].float()\\n                    ], dim=0)\\n                    time_at = torch.zeros(H, batch_size, device=dts.device)\\n                    if H > 1:\\n                        time_at[1:] = torch.cumsum(trans_dts_h, dim=0)[:H-1]\\n                    horizon_mask = (time_at < phys_horizon).unsqueeze(-1).float()\\n                    discount = discount * horizon_mask\\n\\n            transition_dts = torch.cat([init_dt_tensor, dts[:H-2]], 0)\\n\\n            target = tools.lambda_return_variable_dt(\\n                reward[1:],\\n                value[:-1],\\n                discount[1:],\\n                transition_dts,\\n                bootstrap=value[-1],\\n                lambda_=self._config.discount_lambda,\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {\"elapsed_seconds\": 0.5}}"}

delta_min + 1e-8)\\n    preferred_dt = 1.0 + (eff - 1.0) * (1.0 - delta_norm)\\n    dt_indices = torch.arange(1, max_dt + 1, dtype=torch.float32, device=feats.device)\\n    sigma = 1.0\\n    log_probs = -0.5 * ((preferred_dt.unsqueeze(-1) - {"result":"{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"stdout\": \"    def _imagine(self, start, policy, horizon):\\n        dynamics = self._world_model.dynamics\\n        flatten = lambda x: x.reshape([-1] + list(x.shape[2:]))\\n        start = {k: flatten(v) for k, v in start.items()}\\n\\n        if self._config.gate_enabled:\\n            gate = self._world_model.event_gate\\n            config = self._config\\n            step = self._world_model._step\\n\\n            effective_max_dt = self._world_model._effective_max_dt\\n\\n            gate_active = (\\n                gate is not None and self._world_model._gate_scale > 0\\n                and config.gate_fixed_dt == 0\\n            )\\n\\n            def step_fn(prev, _):\\n                state, _, _, prev_dt = prev\\n                feat = dynamics.get_feat(state)\\n                inp = feat.detach()\\n                action = policy(inp).sample()\\n                succ = dynamics.img_step(state, action, dt=prev_dt)\\n                succ_feat = dynamics.get_feat(succ)\\n                if gate_active:\\n                    _, next_dt, _ = gate(succ_feat.detach(), step, hard=True, effective_max_dt=effective_max_dt)\\n                    next_dt = torch.clamp(next_dt, 1, max(1, int(effective_max_dt)))\\n                else:\\n                    batch_size = succ_feat.shape[0]\\n                    fixed = config.gate_fixed_dt if config.gate_fixed_dt > 0 else 1\\n                    next_dt = torch.full(\\n                        (batch_size,), fixed, dtype=torch.long, device=succ_feat.device\\n                    )\\n                return succ, feat, action, next_dt\\n\\n            init_dt = torch.ones(\\n                start[list(start.keys())[0]].shape[0],\\n                dtype=torch.long, device=config.device,\\n            )\\n            if config.gate_fixed_dt > 0:\\n                init_dt = init_dt * config.gate_fixed_dt\\n            succ, feats, actions, dts = tools.static_scan(\\n                step_fn, [torch.arange(horizon)],\\n                (start, None, None, init_dt),\\n            )\\n            states = {\\n                k: torch.cat([start[k][None], v[:-1]], 0)\\n                for k, v in succ.items()\\n            }\\n            return feats, states, actions, dts\\n        else:\\n            def step(prev, _):\\n                state, _, _ = prev\\n                feat = dynamics.get_feat(state)\\n                inp = feat.detach()\\n                action = policy(inp).sample()\\n                succ = dynamics.img_step(state, action)\\n                return succ, feat, action\\n\\n            succ, feats, actions = tools.static_scan(\\n                step, [torch.arange(horizon)], (start, None, None)\\n            )\\n            states = {\\n                k: torch.cat([start[k][None], v[:-1]], 0)\\n                for k, v in succ.items()\\n            }\\n            return feats, states, actions\\n\\n    def _compute_target(self, imag_feat, imag_state, reward, dts=None, init_dt=1.0):\\n        if \\\"cont\\\" in self._world_model.heads:\\n            inp = self._world_model.dynamics.get_feat(imag_state)\\n            discount = self._config.discount * self._world_model.heads[\\\"cont\\\"](inp).mean\\n        else:\\n            discount = self._config.discount * torch.ones_like(reward)\\n        value = self.value(imag_feat).mode()\\n\\n        if dts is not None and self._config.gate_enabled:\\n            H = reward.shape[0]\\n            init_dt_tensor = torch.full_like(dts[:1], init_dt)\\n\\n            horizon_mask = None\\n            if getattr(self._config, 'gate_type', 'gumbel') == 'bernoulli_st':\\n                phys_horizon = getattr(self._config, 'bernoulli_physical_horizon', 30)\\n                if phys_horizon > 0:\\n                    batch_size = dts.shape[1]\\n                    trans_dts_h = torch.cat([\\n                        torch.full((1, batch_size), init_dt, device=dts.device, dtype=torch.float32),\\n                        dts[:H-1].float()\\n                    ], dim=0)\\n                    time_at = torch.zeros(H, batch_size, device=dts.device)\\n                    if H > 1:\\n                        time_at[1:] = torch.cumsum(trans_dts_h, dim=0)[:H-1]\\n                    horizon_mask = (time_at < phys_horizon).unsqueeze(-1).float()\\n                    discount = discount * horizon_mask\\n\\n            transition_dts = torch.cat([init_dt_tensor, dts[:H-2]], 0)\\n\\n            target = tools.lambda_return_variable_dt(\\n                reward[1:],\\n                value[:-1],\\n                discount[1:],\\n                transition_dts,\\n 0)\\n            pcont_adj = discount ** transition_dts_full.unsqueeze(-1).float()\\n            weights = torch.cumprod(\\n                torch.cat([torch.ones_like(pcont_adj[:1]), pcont_adj[:-1]], 0), 0\\n            ).detach()\\n            if horizon_mask is not None:\\n                weights = weights * horizon_mask\\n        else:\\n            target = tools.lambda_return(\\n                reward[1:],\\n                value[:-1],\\n                discount[1:],\\n                bootstrap=value[-1],\\n                lambda_=self._config.discount_lambda,\\n                axis=0,\\n            )\\n            weights = torch.cumprod(\\n                torch.cat([torch.ones_like(discount[:1]), discount[:-1]], 0), 0\\n            ).detach()\\n        return target, weights, value[:-1]\\n\\n    def _compute_actor_loss(\\n        self,\\n        imag_feat,\\n        imag_action,\\n        target,\\n        weights,\\n        base,\\n    ):\\n        metrics = {}\\n        inp = imag_feat.detach()\\n        policy = self.actor(inp)\\n        # Q-val for actor is not transformed using symlog\\n        target = torch.stack(target, dim=1)\\n        if self._config.reward_EMA:\\n            offset, scale = self.reward_ema(target, self.ema_vals)\\n            normed_target = (target - offset) / scale\\n            normed_base = (base - offset) / scale\\n            adv = normed_target - normed_base\\n            metrics.update(tools.tensorstats(normed_target, \\\"normed_target\\\"))\\n            metrics[\\\"EMA_005\\\"] = NotImplementedError(self._config.imag_gradient)\\n        actor_loss = -weights[:-1] * actor_target\\n        return actor_loss, metrics\\n\\n    def _update_slow_target(self):\\n        if self._config.critic[\\\"slow_target\\\"]:\\n            if self._updates % self._config.critic[\\\"slow_target_update\\\"] == 0:\\n                mix = self._config.critic[\\\"slow_target_fraction\\\"]\\n                for s, d in zip(self.value.parameters(), self._slow_value.parameters()):\\n                    d.data = mix * s.data + (1 - mix) * d.data\\n            self._updates += 1\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {\"elapsed_seconds\": 0.35}}"}

             time_at = torch.zeros(H, batch_size, device=dts.device)\\n                    if H > 1:\\n                        time_at[1:] = torch.cumsum(trans_dts_h, dim=0)[:H-1]\\n                    horizon_mask = (time_at < phys_horizon).unsqueeze(-1).float()\\n                    discount = discount * horizon_mask\\n\\n            transition_dts = torch.cat([init_dt_tensor, dts[:H-2]], 0)\\n\\n            target = tools.lambda_return_variable_dt(\\n                reward[1:],\\n                value[:-1],\\n                discount[1:],\\n                transition_dts,\\n                bootstrap=value[-1],\\n                lambda_=self._config.discount_lambda,\\n                axis=0,\\n            )\\n            transition_dts_full = torch.cat([init_dt_tensor, dts[:-1]], 0)\\n            pcont_adj = discount ** transition_dts_full.unsqueeze(-1).float()\\n            weights = torch.cumprod(\\n                torch.cat([torch.ones_like(pcont_adj[:1]), pcont_adj[:-1]], 0), 0\\n            ).detach()\\n            if horizon_mask is not None:\\n                weights = weights * horizon_mask\\n        else:\\n            target = tools.lambda_return(\\n                reward[1:],\\n                value[:-1],\\n                discount[1:],\\n                bootstrap=value[-1],\\n                lambda_=self._config.discount_lambda,\\n                axis=0,\\n            )\\n            weights = torch.cumprod(\\n                torch.cat([torch.ones_like(discount[:1]), discount[:-1]], 0), 0\\n            ).detach()\\n        return target, weights, value[:-1]\\n\\n    def _compute_actor_loss(\\n        self,\\n        imag_feat,\\n        imag_action,\\n        target,\\n        weights,\\n        base,\\n    ):\\n        metrics = {}\\n        inp = imag_feat.detach()\\n        policy = self.actor(inp)\\n        # Q-val for actor is not transformed using symlog\\n        target = torch.stack(target, dim=1)\\n        if self._config.reward_EMA:\\n            offset, scale = self.reward_ema(target, self.ema_vals)\\n            normed_target = (target - offset) / scale\\n            normed_base = (base - offset) / scale\\n            adv = normed_target - normed_base\\n            metrics.update(tools.tensorstats(normed_target, \\\"normed_target\\\"))\\n            metrics[\\\"EMA_005\\\"] = to_np(self.ema_vals[0])\\n            metrics[\\\"EMA_095\\\"] = to_np(self.ema_vals[1])\\n        else:\\n            adv = target - base\\n\\n        if self._config.imag_gradient == \\\"dynamics\\\":\\n            actor_target = adv\\n        elif self._config.imag_gradient == \\\"reinforce\\\":\\n            actor_target = (\\n                policy.log_prob(imag_action)[:-1][:, :, None]\\n                * (target - self.value(imag_feat[:-1]).mode()).detach()\\n            )\\n        elif self._config.imag_gradient == \\\"both\\\":\\n            actor_target = (\\n                policy.log_prob(imag_action)[:-1][:, :, None]\\n                * (target - self.value(imag_feat[:-1]).mode()).detach()\\n            )\\n            mix = self._config.imag_gradient_mix\\n            actor_target = mix * target + (1 - mix) * actor_target\\n            metrics[\\\"imag_gradient_mix\\\"] = mix\\n        else:\\n            raise NotImplementedError(self._config.imag_gradient)\\n        actor_loss = -weights[:-1] * actor_target\\n        return actor_loss, metrics\\n\\n    def _update_slow_target(self):\\n        if self._config.critic[\\\"slow_target\\\"]:\\n            if self._updates % self._config.critic[\\\"slow_target_update\\\"] == 0:\\n                mix = self._config.critic[\\\"slow_target_fraction\\\"]\\n                for s, d in zip(self.value.parameters(), self._slow_value.parameters()):\\n                    d.data = mix * s.data + (1 - mix) * d.data\\n            self._updates += 1\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {\"elapsed_seconds\": 0.35}}"

<details><summary>Logged reasoning block</summary>

```text
Now I see! `RequiresGrad` is very simple:
- `__enter__`: sets `requires_grad_(True)` for the model (e.g., actor)
- `__exit__`: sets `requires_grad_(False)` for the model

It does NOT touch other models' requires_grad. So during actor training:
1. `RequiresGrad(self.actor).__enter__()` → actor params get requires_grad=True
2. The world model params (including dt_embedding) keep whatever requires_grad they already have


3. After `__exit__()` → actor params get requires_grad=False

The key question is what state dt_embedding is in during actor training. Looking at the training flow, the world model gets trained first with its own optimizer, then actor/critic training happens. If `RequiresGrad(world_model).__exit__()` is called after world model training, all world model params including dt_embedding would have requires_grad=False, so gradients from actor loss wouldn't flow through it anyway. Let me check the models.py file to see how this actually plays out.

The actor holds a reference to the world model and uses its dynamics during imagination. In standard DreamerV3, all modules start with requires_grad=False after their training phase exits. So during actor training, the actor's parameters are True but the world model's parameters are False. However, the computation graph still flows through the world model during imagination, which means I need to trace whether gradients actually propagate through dt_embedding or if it's blocked by the requires_grad=False setting. exists. Even though WM params have requires_grad=False, the activations (intermediate tensors) still require grad if they're derived from grad-requiring inputs.

Wait, no. If a parameter has `requires_grad=False`, then tensors computed from it won't require grad (unless they also depend on other tensors that do require grad). The dt_embedding weight has requires_grad=False during actor training, so `dt_emb = self._dt_embedding(dt - 1)` would produce a tensor that doesn't require grad (since dt is an integer tensor, not requiring grad). So the gradient wouldn't flow through dt_emb anyway.

But wait, the RSSM hidden state (deter) is computed through the GRU cell, which depends on RSSM parameters. If those params have requires_grad=False, then the GRU output wouldn't require grad either...

Actually, I'm realizing the actor IS trained with dynamics gradients, where the loss flows backward through the value function and into the imagined features. For this to work, the imagined features need to be part of the computation graph connected to the world model. But if world model parameters have requires_grad=False, that shouldn't prevent the computation graph from existing—it just means those parameters won't receive updates. The key distinction is that `requires_grad` on parameters controls whether they accumulate gradients, not whether operations involving them are tracked in the graph. from these parameters. In PyTorch, even if a parameter has `requires_grad=False`, if you compute a tensor from it and other tensors that DO require grad, the result will require grad. BUT — if NO input to an operation requires grad, the output won't require grad either.

Let me trace through the computation graph during actor training. The `start` state comes from the world model's real-data inference and is likely detached. In `_imagine`, when I call `dynamics.img_step(state, action, dt=prev_dt)`, the `state` is the previous RSSM state (initially `start` which is detached, then the previous step's output), and the `action` comes from `policy(inp).sample()` where `inp = feat.detach()`. Even though the input features are detached, the sampled action requires grad through the policy parameters via reparameterization.

Inside `img_step`, the action gets concatenated with other tensors and passed through layers that have frozen parameters. Since the action requires grad, the output of those layers will also require grad regardless of whether the layer parameters are frozen. The GRU cell receives this gradient-requiring input, so its output `deter` also requires grad. This means `imag_feat` requires grad through the actor's action choices, creating a gradient path from the actor loss back through the value function, the imagined features, the dynamics step, and finally to the actor parameters.

The key insight is that gradients flow through the world model's frozen computations as if it were a fixed function, but only the actor parameters actually get updated. For the `dt_embedding`, since it has `requires_grad=False` and receives a long tensor index input, the embedding output doesn't require grad. When concatenated with other tensors, it acts like a constant — gradients flow around it but not through it. So whether I explicitly detach `dt_emb` or not doesn't matter for actor training, since the embedding parameters are already frozen.

But I should double-check how the training loop actually handles the world model's gradient state to make sure I'm understanding this correctly.

{"result":"{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"stdout\": \"# Event-Gated Adaptive-Horizon DreamerV3 — World Model & Behavior\\n# Modified from NM512/dreamerv3-torch.\\n# Changes: WorldModel integrates EventGate + variable-dt sub-sampling;\\n#          ImagBehavior uses gate-driven dt in imagination + adapted lambda-returns.\\n\\nimport copy\\nimport math\\n\\nimport torch\\nfrom torch import nn\\nfrom torch.nn import functional as F\\n\\nimport networks\\nimport tools\\n\\nto_np = lambda x: x.detach().cpu().numpy()\\n\\n\\ndef compute_gate_targets(feats, max_dt, effective_max_dt=None):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Soft supervision target for the event gate derived from state deltas.\\n\\n    Large delta -> prefer small dt (index 0 = dt=1).\\n    Small delta -> prefer large dt (index K-1 = dt=K).\\n    Probability mass is placed only within [1, effective_max_dt].\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    if effective_max_dt is None:\\n        effective_max_dt = max_dt\\n    eff = max(1, int(effective_max_dt))\\n    feats = feats.detach()\\n    deltas = torch.norm(feats[1:] - feats[:-1], dim=-1)  # (time-1, batch)\\n    delta_min = deltas.min(dim=0, keepdim=True).values\\n    delta_max = deltas.max(dim=0, keepdim=True).values\\n    delta_norm = (deltas - delta_min) / (delta_max - delta_min + 1e-8)\\n    preferred_dt = 1.0 + (eff - 1.0) * (1.0 - delta_norm)\\n    dt_indices = torch.arange(1, max_dt + 1, dtype=torch.float32, device=feats.device)\\n    sigma = 1.0\\n    log_probs = -0.5 * ((preferred_dt.unsqueeze(-1) - dt_indices) / sigma) ** 2\\n    if eff < max_dt:\\n        log_probs[..., eff:] = -1e9\\n    target_dist = F.softmax(log_probs, dim=-1)\\n    return target_dist\\n\\n\\ndef gate_loss_fn(gate_logits, target_dist):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Cross-entropy between gate predictions and supervision target.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    log_probs = F.log_softmax(gate_logits, dim=-1)\\n    return -(target_dist * log_probs).sum(dim=-1)\\n\\n\\nclass RewardEMA:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"running mean and std\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\n    def __init__(self, device, alpha=1e-2):\\n        self.device = device\\n        self.alpha = alpha\\n        self.range = torch.tensor([0.05, 0.95], device=device)\\n\\n    def __call__(self, x, ema_vals):\\n        flat_x = torch.flatten(x.detach())\\n        x_quantile = torch.quantile(input=flat_x, q=self.range)\\n        # this should be in-place operation\\n        ema_vals[:] = self.alpha * x_quantile + (1 - Now I see: `ImagBehavior.__init__` has `self._world_model = world_model` — this is stored as an attribute, NOT as a registered submodule (it's not using `self.world_model = world_model` pattern that would register it as a submodule since it starts with `_`). 

Wait, in PyTorch, if you set `self._world_model = world_model` where world_model is an nn.Module, it IS registered as a submodule (PyTorch's __setattr__ handles this). Let me verify... Actually yes, PyTorch registers any nn.Module assigned to a module attribute, regardless of the name.


So `ImagBehavior` contains the actor, value, and the entire world model as submodules. That means when `RequiresGrad(self)` is applied, it enables gradients for all parameters across all three components. But looking at the actual optimizer calls, they're only passing `self.actor.parameters()` and `self.value.parameters()` — so the world model parameters are being excluded from the optimization step even though gradients are enabled for them.

During the imagination rollout inside `RequiresGrad(self.actor)`, only actor parameters have gradients enabled, while world model parameters (including dt_embedding) have gradients disabled from the previous context manager exit. I need to trace through the full training sequence to understand how the gradient states flow between the world model training phase and the imagination phase. WM params requires_grad=False

2. `ImagBehavior._train(start, reward)`:
   - Line 487: `with tools.RequiresGrad(self.actor):` → actor params requires_grad=True
   - Inside: imagination, compute losses
   - Exit: actor params requires_grad=False
   - Line 518: `with tools.RequiresGrad(self.value):` → value params requires_grad=True
   - Inside: compute value loss
   - Exit: value params requires_grad=False
   - Line 592: `with tools.RequiresGrad(self):` → ALL ImagBehavior params requires_grad=True (actor, value, AND world_model!)
   - Call actor_opt and value_opt
   - Exit: ALL ImagBehavior params requires_grad=False

So during imagination, the actor has gradients enabled while the value and world model stay frozen. This means dt_embedding won't require grad, so the `.detach()` call is redundant. But even though the embedding itself doesn't update, the dt values still influence the feature representations that the critic learns from, which could be driving the CIVO effect. To isolate whether dt information is actually causing the problem, I'd need to either zero out the dt embeddings or use a fixed placeholder instead.

The researcher's instruction to add detach makes sense as a first test—if it shows no difference, that confirms the gradient path isn't the issue. But re-reading the Chinese description more carefully, "critic 无法利用精确的 dt 信息" suggests the goal is preventing the critic from accessing precise dt information altogether, which might require more than just detaching.

Let me implement the detach as requested and add a note about the gradient flow analysis. The experiment will reveal whether this alone achieves the intended effect or if something stronger is needed. Since dt_embedding has requires_grad=False during imagination anyway, detaching the dt_emb values in the computation graph should prevent any gradient flow through that path.

{
  "server": "bjb1-45299",
  "command": "cd /root/autodl-tmp/event-gated-adaptive-horizon && python3 -c \"\nimport re\n\n# === 1. configs.yaml: add critic_dt_emb_detach ===\nwith open('configs.yaml', 'r') as f:\n    cfg = f.read()\n\n# Insert after bernoulli_physical_horizon line\ncfg = cfg.replace(\n    '  bernoulli_physical_horizon: 30\\n',\n    '  bernoulli_physical_horizon: 30\\n  critic_dt_emb_detach: false\\n'\n)\nwith open('configs.yaml', 'w') as f:\n    f.write(cfg)\nprint('configs.yaml updated')\n\n# === 2. networks.py: add detach_dt_emb to img_step ===\nwith open('networks.py', 'r') as f:\n    net = f.read()\n\n# Modify img_step signature\nnet = net.replace(\n    'def img_step(self, prev_state, prev_action, dt=None, sample=True):',\n    'def img_step(self, prev_state, prev_action, dt=None, sample=True, detach_dt_emb=False):'\n)\n\n# Add detach after dt_emb computation\nnet = net.replace(\n    '            dt_emb = self._dt_embedding(dt - 1)\\n            x = torch.cat([prev_stoch, prev_action, dt_emb], -1)',\n    '            dt_emb = self._dt_embedding(dt - 1)\\n            if detach_dt_emb:\\n                dt_emb = dt_emb.detach()\\n            x = torch.cat([prev_stoch, prev_action, dt_emb], -1)'\n)\nwith open('networks.py', 'w') as f:\n    f.write(net)\nprint('networks.py updated')\n\n# === 3. models.py: pass detach flag in _imagine ===\nwith open('models.py', 'r') as f:\n    mdl = f.read()\n\n# In _imagine, add detach_dt flag and pass to img_step\nmdl = mdl.replace(\n    '''            gate_active = (\n                gate is not None and self._world_model._gate_scale > 0\n                and config.gate_fixed_dt == 0\n            )''',\n    '''            detach_dt = getattr(config, 'critic_dt_emb_detach', False)\n\n            gate_active = (\n                gate is not None and self._world_model._gate_scale > 0\n                and config.gate_fixed_dt == 0\n            )'''\n)\n\n# Pass detach_dt_emb in the gate-enabled img_step call\nmdl = mdl.replace(\n    '                succ = dynamics.img_step(state, action, dt=prev_dt)\\n                succ_feat = dynamics.get_feat(succ)',\n    '                succ = dynamics.img_step(state, action, dt=prev_dt, detach_dt_emb=detach_dt)\\n                succ_feat = dynamics.get_feat(succ)'\n)\n\nwith open('models.py', 'w') as f:\n    f.write(mdl)\nprint('models.py updated')\n\"\n",
  "account": "root"
}
```

initial=\\\"learned\\\",\\n        num_actions=None,\\n        embed=None,\\n        device=None,\\n        max_dt=1,\\n        dt_embed_dim=32,\\n    ):\\n        super(RSSM, self).__init__()\\n        self._stoch = stoch\\n        self._deter = deter\\n        self._hidden = hidden\\n        self._min_std = min_std\\n        self._rec_depth = rec_depth\\n        self._discrete = discrete\\n        act = getattr(torch.nn, act)\\n        self._mean_act = mean_act\\n        self._std_act = std_act\\n        self._unimix_ratio = unimix_ratio\\n        self._initial = initial\\n        self._num_actions = num_actions\\n        self._embed = embed\\n        self._device = device\\n\\n        self._max_dt = max_dt\\n        self._dt_embed_dim = dt_embed_dim\\n        if self._max_dt > zip(self.value.parameters(), self._slow_value.parameters()):\\n                    d.data = mix * s.data + (1 - mix) * d.data\\n            self._updates += 1\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {\"elapsed_seconds\": 0.42}}"
    }
  }
}

Since `prefill=100` means collecting 100 environment steps (but with action_repeat=2, that's 50 actual steps × 1000 time_limit environment, so it needs to collect 100*action_repeat=200 raw env steps, which at 1 env is sequential). Actually, `prefill=100` means 100 buffer entries = 200 raw env frames with action_repeat=2. The walker env with 1000 time_limit has 500 steps per episode. 

With prefill=100 and action_repeat=2, I only need 100 transitions—less than a single episode. The default prefill is actually 2500, so setting it to 100 should speed things up considerably, though the stuck output is probably just buffering or the environment still initializing. {
  "name": "plan_015_submit_command",
  "type": "intermediate",
  "description": "plan_015 critic dt_emb detach experiment — ready-to-execute training command (105K steps, walker_walk, 008d config + critic_dt_emb_detach=True)",
  "project": "event-gated-adaptive-horizon",
  "exp_id": "exp_015",
  "content": "# plan_015: Critic dt_embedding detach experiment\n# Purpose: Test whether trained dt_embedding is a CIVO vulnerability source\n# Method: detach dt_emb in imagination img_step, so critic can't exploit dt gradients\n# Base config: same as exp_008d (subsample ON, dt_embed_dim=32, fixed_dt=2, H=8)\n\n# === Submission command (run on cuda:1 after 009e completes) ===\n\nsource /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh && conda activate base && export LD_PRELOAD=/usr/lib/x86_64-linux-gnu/libstdc++.so.6 && export MUJOCO_GL=osmesa && export PYOPENGL_PLATFORM=osmesa && cd /root/autodl-tmp/event-gated-adaptive-horizon && nohup python -u dreamer.py --configs defaults dmc_proprio --task dmc_walker_walk --logdir logdir/exp_015_critic_dt_detach --steps 105000 --seed 1 --compile False --device cuda:1 --gate_enabled True --gate_fixed_dt 2 --gate_subsample_max_dt 2 --imag_horizon 8 --critic_dt_emb_detach True > logdir/exp_015_critic_dt_detach.log 2>&1 &\n\n# === Code changes summary ===\n# 1. configs.yaml:119 — added `critic_dt_emb_detach: false` (default off)\n# 2. networks.py:241 — RSSM.img_step() accepts `detach_dt_emb=False` parameter\n# 3. networks.py:255-257 — when detach_dt_emb=True, dt_emb = dt_emb.detach()\n# 4. models.py:609 — reads config flag: detach_dt = getattr(config, 'critic_dt_emb_detach', False)\n# 5. models.py:621 — passes detach_dt_emb=detach_dt to dynamics.img_step() in _imagine()\n\n# === Backup files ===\n# networks.py.bak.pre_plan015\n# models.py.bak.pre_plan015\n# configs.yaml.bak.pre_plan015\n",
  "content_filename": "plan_015_submit_command.sh"
}
```
